    "new_string": "import os\nimport sys\nimport streamlit as st\nimport json\nimport ast\nimport time\nimport traceback\nimport base64\nimport re\nimport requests\nfrom dotenv import load_dotenv\n\n# Load environment variables from .env file\nload_dotenv()\n\n# --- PATH INJECTION ---\nroot_dir = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))\nif root_dir not in sys.path: sys.path.insert(0, root_dir)\nsrc_dir = os.path.join(root_dir, \"src\")\nif src_dir not in sys.path: sys.path.insert(0, src_dir)\n\n# --- INTERNAL IMPORTS ---\nfrom src.utils.diagnostics import get_logger, log_system_info\nfrom src.api.client import APIClient\nfrom src.ui.recorder import record_audio\nfrom src.utils.file_manager import FileManager\nfrom src.utils.history import HistoryManager\nfrom src.backend.hardware import HardwareInfo\nfrom src.backend.personas import Personas\nfrom src.utils.text_processor import clean_llm_text, parse_file\nfrom src.ui.dashboard import render_final_analysis, render_history_dashboard\n\n# --- INITIALIZATION ---\nif 'sys_logged' not in st.session_state:\n    log_system_info()\n    st.session_state['sys_logged'] = True\n\nlogger = get_logger()\nFileManager.initialize_directories()\n\n# --- CACHED WRAPPERS ---\n@st.cache_data(ttl=300)\ndef get_cached_models():\n    return APIClient.get_local_models()\n\n# --- UI COMPONENTS ---\n\n@st.fragment(run_every=2)\ndef render_resource_usage():\n    \"\"\"Isolated fragment for high-frequency resource monitoring.\"\"\"\n    hw_status = APIClient.get_hardware_status()\n    st.subheader(\"🖥️ Resource Usage\")\n    if hw_status:\n        stats = hw_status.get(\"stats\", {})\n        c_v, r_p = stats.get('cpu_percent', 0), stats.get('ram_percent', 0)\n        r_u, r_t = stats.get('ram_used_gb', 0), stats.get('ram_total_gb', 0)\n        st.progress(min(max(float(c_v) / 100.0, 0.0), 1.0), text=f\"CPU: {c_v}%\")\n        st.progress(min(max(float(r_p) / 100.0, 0.0), 1.0), text=f\"RAM: {r_u}/{r_t} GB\")\n        if stats.get(\"gpu_detected\"):\n            v_p, v_u, v_t = stats.get('vram_percent', 0), stats.get('vram_used_gb', 0), stats.get('vram_total_gb', 0)\n            st.progress(min(max(float(v_p) / 100.0, 0.0), 1.0), text=f\"GPU VRAM: {v_u}/{v_t} GB\")\n    else:\n        st.error(\"🔴 Backend: Offline\")\n\n@st.fragment(run_every=5)\ndef render_api_panel(w_n):\n    \"\"\"Dedicated section for AI Engine selection and live connection status.\"\"\"\n    st.subheader(\"🔌 API & Inference\")\n    \n    providers = [\"Ollama (Local)\", \"OpenAI\", \"Google Gemini\", \"Anthropic\"]\n    default_provider = st.session_state.get('default_provider', \"Google Gemini\")\n    default_idx = providers.index(default_provider)\n    provider = st.selectbox(\"Inference Provider\", providers, index=default_idx, key=f\"prov_sel_{w_n}\")\n    \n    if provider == \"Ollama (Local)\":\n        models = get_cached_models()\n        selected_model = st.selectbox(\"Local Model\", models, key=f\"model_sel_{w_n}\")\n        with st.expander(\"⬇️ Download Model\"):\n            tag = st.selectbox(\"Tag\", [\"llama3.1:8b\", \"gemma2:9b\", \"mistral:7b\", \"-- Other --\"], key=f\"pull_tag_{w_n}\")\n            if tag == \"-- Other --\": tag = st.text_input(\"Custom Tag\", key=f\"pull_custom_{w_n}\")\n            if st.button(\"Pull Model\", use_container_width=True):\n                p = st.progress(0); res = APIClient.pull_model_stream(tag)\n                for line in res.iter_lines():\n                    if line:\n                        d = json.loads(line.decode('utf-8'))\n                        if \"completed\" in d and \"total\" in d and d[\"total\"] > 0: p.progress(d[\"completed\"]/d[\"total\"])\n                st.success(\"Ready!\"); st.cache_data.clear(); st.rerun()\n        api_key = None\n    else:\n        m_map = {\"OpenAI\": [\"gpt-5.4-nano\", \"gpt-5-nano\", \"o4-mini\", \"gpt-4o-mini\"], \"Anthropic\": [\"claude-sonnet-4-6\",\"claude-haiku-4-5\", \"claude-sonnet-4-5\", \"claude-sonnet-4-0\"], \"Google Gemini\": [\"gemini-3-flash-preview\", \"gemini-3.1-flash-lite-preview\", \"gemini-2.5-flash-lite\"]}\n        selected_model = st.selectbox(\"Model\", m_map.get(provider, [\"-- Other --\"]) + [\"-- Other --\"], key=f\"ext_model_{w_n}\")\n        if selected_model == \"-- Other --\": selected_model = st.text_input(\"Custom Model\", key=f\"ext_custom_{w_n}\")\n        \n        key_links = {\"OpenAI\": \"https://platform.openai.com/api-keys\", \"Anthropic\": \"https://platform.claude.com/settings/keys\", \"Google Gemini\": \"https://aistudio.google.com/app/api-keys\"}\n        st.caption(f\"ℹ️ [Get {provider} Key]({key_links.get(provider, '#')})\")\n\n        # UNIQUE KEY PER PROVIDER: This ensures fields are isolated and don't mix up keys\n        api_key_key = f\"api_key_{provider}_{w_n}\"\n        api_key = st.text_input(\"Secret API Key\", value=st.session_state['saved_keys'].get(provider, \"\"), type=\"password\", key=api_key_key)\n\n        if api_key != st.session_state['saved_keys'].get(provider):\n            st.session_state['saved_keys'][provider] = api_key\n            FileManager.save_keys(st.session_state['saved_keys'])\n    # Sync Config\n    compute_target = st.session_state.get(f\"comp_target_{w_n}\", \"CPU & RAM Core\")\n    st.session_state['engine_config'] = {\"provider\": provider, \"model\": selected_model, \"compute\": compute_target, \"api_key\": api_key}\n\n    # Connection Status Badges\n    st.write(\"\")\n    hw_status = APIClient.get_hardware_status()\n    if hw_status:\n        st.success(\"🟢 Backend: Online\")\n        success, msg = APIClient.test_connection(st.session_state['engine_config'])\n        if success: st.success(\"🟢 AI Engine: Ready\")\n        else: st.error(msg)\n    else:\n        st.error(\"🔴 Backend: Offline\")\n\n@st.fragment\ndef render_config_panel(w_n):\n    \"\"\"Isolated fragment for hardware and voice configuration.\"\"\"\n    st.subheader(\"⚙️ Configuration\")\n    \n    # 1. Hardware Detection & Selection\n    hw = HardwareInfo()\n    rec_mode, rec_text = hw.get_recommendation()\n    \n    # Detect and show physical hardware names\n    hw_status = APIClient.get_hardware_status()\n    det_hw = hw_status.get(\"detected_hw\", \"Standard CPU\") if hw_status else \"Scanning...\"\n    \n    c_options = [\"NVIDIA GPU\", \"CPU & RAM Core\"]\n    if hw.is_apple_silicon:\n        c_options = [\"Apple Silicon\", \"CPU & RAM Core\"]\n    \n    compute_target = st.radio(\"Compute Allocation\", c_options, horizontal=True, key=f\"comp_target_{w_n}\")\n    \n    # Show ALL detected hardware\n    st.caption(f\"**Detected Hardware:** {det_hw}\")\n    if hw.has_nvidia and hw.is_apple_silicon: # Rare edge case\n        st.caption(\"🔍 Multiple accelerators found (NVIDIA + M-Series)\")\n    \n    with st.expander(\"💡 Hardware Helper\"):\n        st.info(rec_text)\n\n    # 2. Voice Selection\n    v_opt = st.radio(\"Coach Voice\", [\"Male\", \"Female\"], horizontal=True, key=f\"v_opt_{w_n}\")\n    st.session_state['selected_voice'] = \"en-US-GuyNeural\" if v_opt == \"Male\" else \"en-US-AvaNeural\"\n\n    # 3. Active Mode Logic (Shows real-time backend usage)\n    if hw_status:\n        actual_using = \"Standard CPU (Int8)\"\n        if compute_target == \"NVIDIA GPU\" and hw_status.get(\"has_nvidia\"):\n            actual_using = \"NVIDIA GPU (FP16)\"\n        elif compute_target == \"Apple Silicon\" and hw_status.get(\"is_apple_silicon\"):\n            actual_using = \"Apple Neural Engine (FP32)\"\n        st.info(f\"**Active Mode:** {actual_using}\")\n\ndef trigger_voice(text):\n    \"\"\"Fetches audio and sets play state.\"\"\"\n    if not text: return\n    clean_text = clean_llm_text(text)\n    voice = st.session_state.get('selected_voice', \"en-US-GuyNeural\")\n    audio_bytes = APIClient.generate_speech(clean_text, voice=voice)\n    if audio_bytes:\n        st.session_state['play_now_bytes'] = audio_bytes\n        st.session_state['audio_nonce'] = st.session_state.get('audio_nonce', 0) + 1\n        return True\n    return False\n\n@st.fragment\ndef render_interview_loop(info):\n    \"\"\"Isolated interview loop for real-time interaction without page flickering.\"\"\"\n    st.subheader(\"🎙️ Live Interview Simulator\")\n    st.info(f\"⏱️ **Stage:** {info['meaning']} | **Interviewer:** {info['persona']}\")\n    \n    for idx, msg in enumerate(st.session_state['chat_history']):\n        with st.chat_message(msg[\"role\"], avatar=(\"🤖\" if msg[\"role\"] == \"assistant\" else \"👤\")):\n            st.write(msg[\"content\"])\n            if msg[\"role\"] == \"assistant\":\n                if st.button(\"🔊 Replay\", key=f\"replay_{idx}\"):\n                    trigger_voice(msg[\"content\"]); st.rerun()\n\n    st.divider()\n    if 'rec_nonce' not in st.session_state: st.session_state['rec_nonce'] = 0\n    audio_path = record_audio(key=f\"recorder_{st.session_state['rec_nonce']}\")\n    \n    c_sub, c_end = st.columns(2)\n    compute_type = st.session_state.get('engine_config', {}).get('compute', 'CPU & RAM Core')\n    \n    if audio_path and c_sub.button(\"🗣️ Submit Answer\", type=\"primary\", use_container_width=True):\n        with st.spinner(\"Processing...\"):\n            t, m, d, e = APIClient.process_audio(audio_path, info['recommended_mode'], compute_type)\n            if not e:\n                st.session_state['aggregated_metrics'].append({\"transcript\": t, \"metrics\": m, \"duration\": d})\n                st.session_state['chat_history'].append({\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": t})\n                with st.spinner(\"Thinking...\"):\n                    nxt = APIClient.generate_response(st.session_state['sys_p'], t, st.session_state['chat_history'][:-1], st.session_state['engine_config'], resume_context=st.session_state.get('resume_text', \"\"), job_context=st.session_state.get('job_desc_text', \"\"))\n                    trigger_voice(nxt)\n                    st.session_state['chat_history'].append({\"role\": \"assistant\", \"content\": clean_llm_text(nxt)})\n                    st.session_state['rec_nonce'] += 1\n                    st.rerun()\n\n    if c_end.button(\"🛑 End Interview & Analyze\", use_container_width=True):\n        if audio_path:\n            with st.spinner(\"Finalizing...\"):\n                t, m, d, e = APIClient.process_audio(audio_path, info['recommended_mode'], compute_type)\n                if not e:\n                    st.session_state['aggregated_metrics'].append({\"transcript\": t, \"metrics\": m, \"duration\": d})\n                    st.session_state['chat_history'].append({\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": t})\n        st.session_state['interview_complete'] = True; st.rerun()\n\n@st.fragment\ndef render_setup_wizard(w_n):\n    \"\"\"Isolated setup wizard for role definition and document processing.\"\"\"\n    with st.expander(\"🛠️ Interview Setup Wizard\", expanded=(st.session_state['setup_step'] < 3)):\n        st.markdown(\"### 1. Define Target Role\")\n        c1, c2, c3 = st.columns(3)\n        ind = c1.text_input(\"Industry\", placeholder=\"e.g., Tech\", key=f\"ind_in_{w_n}\")\n        role = c2.text_input(\"Job Title\", placeholder=\"e.g., Backend Developer\", key=f\"role_in_{w_n}\")\n        sen = c3.selectbox(\"Seniority\", [\"Entry-Level\", \"Mid-Level\", \"Senior / Lead\", \"Executive\"], key=f\"sen_in_{w_n}\")\n        \n        st.divider()\n        st.markdown(\"### 2. Contextual Data (Optional)\")\n        c_res, c_jd = st.columns(2)\n        res_f = c_res.file_uploader(\"Upload Resume\", type=[\"pdf\", \"txt\", \"docx\"], key=f\"res_up_{w_n}\")\n        jd_f = c_jd.file_uploader(\"Upload Job Description\", type=[\"pdf\", \"txt\", \"docx\"], key=f\"jd_up_{w_n}\")\n        \n        if res_f:\n            res_text = parse_file(res_f)\n            if res_text: st.session_state['resume_text'] = res_text\n            else: st.warning(f\"⚠️ Could not read resume: {res_f.name}\"); st.session_state['resume_text'] = None\n        else: st.session_state['resume_text'] = \"\"\n\n        if jd_f:\n            jd_text = parse_file(jd_f)\n            if jd_text: st.session_state['job_desc_text'] = jd_text\n            else: st.warning(f\"⚠️ Could not read job description: {jd_f.name}\"); st.session_state['job_desc_text'] = None\n        else: st.session_state['job_desc_text'] = \"\"\n\n        if st.button(\"Generate Interview Rounds\", disabled=not (ind and role), use_container_width=True):\n            hw_status = APIClient.get_hardware_status()\n            if not hw_status: st.error(\"🔴 Backend Offline\")\n            else:\n                success, msg = APIClient.test_connection(st.session_state['engine_config'])\n                if not success: st.error(f\"❌ Connection Error: {msg}\")\n                else:\n                    with st.spinner(\"Structuring...\"):\n                        p = (f\"List 4 unique interview rounds for a {sen} {role} in the {ind} industry. Output ONLY a Python list of strings.\")\n                        resp = APIClient.generate_response(\"Expert recruiter. Return ONLY a Python list.\", p, [], st.session_state['engine_config'])\n                        try:\n                            import re; m = re.search(r\"\\[.*\\]\", resp, re.DOTALL)\n                            rounds = ast.literal_eval(m.group()) if m else [resp]\n                            st.session_state['rounds'] = [str(r).strip() for r in rounds if len(str(r)) < 150]\n                        except: st.session_state['rounds'] = [\"1. Initial Screen\", \"2. Technical Round\", \"3. Culture Fit\", \"4. Final Manager\"]\n                        st.session_state['setup_step'] = 2; st.rerun()\n\n        if st.session_state['setup_step'] >= 2:\n            st.divider()\n            st.markdown(\"### 3. Select Stage & Persona\")\n            sel_r = st.selectbox(\"Target Interview Round:\", st.session_state['rounds'], key=f\"round_sel_{w_n}\")\n            sel_p = st.selectbox(\"Interviewer Style:\", list(Personas.PERSONA_PROMPTS.keys()), key=f\"persona_sel_{w_n}\")\n            if st.button(\"Start Interview Simulation\", type=\"primary\", use_container_width=True):\n                if not sel_r: st.error(\"Please generate interview rounds in Step 1 first!\")\n                else:\n                    with st.spinner(\"Writing questions...\"):\n                        st.session_state.update({'selected_persona_label': sel_p, 'selected_round': sel_r, 'seniority': sen, 'job_title': role, 'industry': ind})\n                        persona_cfg = Personas.get_interviewer_by_type(sel_r.split(\" \")[0], sen)\n                        st.session_state['round_info'] = {\"meaning\": persona_cfg['meaning'], \"persona\": persona_cfg['persona'], \"recommended_mode\": \"Balanced\"}\n                        q_resp = APIClient.generate_questions(sen, role, ind, sel_r, st.session_state['engine_config'], st.session_state['resume_text'], st.session_state['job_desc_text'])\n                        raw_qs = [q.strip() for q in q_resp.split('\\n') if len(q.strip()) > 10]\n                        st.session_state['custom_questions'] = [clean_llm_text(q) for q in raw_qs]\n                        st.session_state['setup_step'] = 3; st.rerun()\n\n# --- MAIN APP ---\ndef main():\n    try:\n        if 'wipe_nonce' not in st.session_state: st.session_state['wipe_nonce'] = 0\n        w_n = st.session_state['wipe_nonce']\n\n        if 'default_provider' not in st.session_state:\n            try:\n                res = requests.get(\"http://127.0.0.1:11434/api/tags\", timeout=1)\n                if res.status_code == 200: st.session_state['default_provider'] = \"Ollama (Local)\"\n                else: st.session_state['default_provider'] = \"Google Gemini\"\n            except: st.session_state['default_provider'] = \"Google Gemini\"\n\n        if 'saved_keys' not in st.session_state:\n            st.session_state['saved_keys'] = FileManager.load_saved_keys()\n\n        # --- SIDEBAR ---\n        with st.sidebar:\n            st.image(\"assets/Data-Drifters.png\", width=\"stretch\")\n            \n            # 1. Resource Usage (Top)\n            render_resource_usage()\n            st.divider()\n            \n            # 2. API & Inference (Middle)\n            render_api_panel(w_n)\n            st.divider()\n            \n            # 3. Configuration (Bottom - Now a Fragment)\n            render_config_panel(w_n)\n            \n            st.divider()\n            if st.button(\"🔄 Start New Interview\", use_container_width=True):\n                new_nonce = st.session_state.get('wipe_nonce', 0) + 1\n                keep = ['engine_config', 'sys_logged', 'selected_voice', 'saved_keys', 'default_provider']\n                for k in list(st.session_state.keys()):\n                    if k not in keep: del st.session_state[k]\n                st.session_state['wipe_nonce'] = new_nonce; st.rerun()\n\n            with st.expander(\"🗑️ Danger Zone\"):\n                if st.button(\"Delete All Data\", type=\"primary\", use_container_width=True):\n                    FileManager.cleanup_all_data(); HistoryManager.clear_history()\n                    FileManager.safe_delete_file(os.path.join(FileManager.TEMP_DIR, \"vault.json\"))\n                    new_nonce = st.session_state.get('wipe_nonce', 0) + 1\n                    for k in list(st.session_state.keys()): del st.session_state[k]\n                    st.session_state['wipe_nonce'] = new_nonce; st.rerun()\n\n        st.title(\"🎙️ AI Interview Coach\")\n\n        if st.session_state.get('play_now_bytes'):\n            b64 = base64.b64encode(st.session_state['play_now_bytes']).decode()\n            n = st.session_state.get('audio_nonce', 0)\n            st.components.v1.html(f'<audio id=\"a_{n}\" autoplay><source src=\"data:audio/mp3;base64,{b64}\" type=\"audio/mp3\"></audio><script>document.getElementById(\"a_{n}\").play();</script>', height=1)\n            st.session_state['play_now_bytes'] = None\n\n        tab_coach, tab_history = st.tabs([\"🎯 Live Coach\", \"📈 Session History\"])\n        \n        with tab_coach:\n            if 'setup_step' not in st.session_state: st.session_state['setup_step'] = 1\n            if 'rounds' not in st.session_state: st.session_state['rounds'] = []\n            if 'aggregated_metrics' not in st.session_state: st.session_state['aggregated_metrics'] = []\n            \n            if st.session_state['setup_step'] < 3: render_setup_wizard(w_n)\n\n            if st.session_state['setup_step'] == 3 and not st.session_state.get('interview_complete'):\n                # (Logic handled in fragments)\n                pass\n\n            if st.session_state['setup_step'] == 3 and not st.session_state.get('interview_complete'):\n                if 'chat_history' not in st.session_state:\n                    st.session_state['sys_p'] = Personas.get_interview_sys_prompt(st.session_state['selected_persona_label'], st.session_state['selected_round'], st.session_state['seniority'], st.session_state['job_title'], st.session_state['industry'], st.session_state['resume_text'], st.session_state['job_desc_text'])\n                    st.session_state['chat_history'] = []\n                    with st.spinner(\"🎙️ Coach is entering the room...\"):\n                        first_q = APIClient.generate_response(st.session_state['sys_p'], \"Start the interview. Greet me and ask ONLY your first question.\", [], st.session_state['engine_config'], resume_context=st.session_state.get('resume_text', \"\"), job_context=st.session_state.get('job_desc_text', \"\"))\n                        first_q = clean_llm_text(first_q); trigger_voice(first_q)\n                        st.session_state['chat_history'].append({\"role\": \"assistant\", \"content\": first_q}); st.rerun()\n                render_interview_loop(st.session_state['round_info'])\n\n            if st.session_state.get('interview_complete'):\n                if 'final_feedback' not in st.session_state:\n                    with st.spinner(\"Analyzing...\"):\n                        v = len(st.session_state['aggregated_metrics'])\n                        t_w = sum([m['metrics']['wpm'] for m in st.session_state['aggregated_metrics']])\n                        t_f = sum([m['metrics']['filler_count'] for m in st.session_state['aggregated_metrics']])\n                        t_d = sum([m['duration'] for m in st.session_state['aggregated_metrics']])\n                        \n                        # New Metrics Aggregation\n                        t_p = sum([m['metrics'].get('pause_count', 0) for m in st.session_state['aggregated_metrics']])\n                        t_b = sum([m['metrics'].get('blunder_count', 0) for m in st.session_state['aggregated_metrics']])\n                        tones = [m['metrics'].get('tone_label', 'Neutral') for m in st.session_state['aggregated_metrics']]\n                        dom_tone = max(set(tones), key=tones.count) if tones else \"Neutral\"\n                        \n                        avg_w = t_w / v if v > 0 else 0; full_t = \"\"\n                        for msg in st.session_state['chat_history']: full_t += f\"<b>{'Interviewer' if msg['role'] == 'assistant' else 'Candidate'}:</b> {msg['content']}<br><br>\\n\"\n                        f_p = Personas.get_final_feedback_prompt(st.session_state['seniority'], st.session_state['job_title'], st.session_state['industry'], full_t)\n                        f_f = APIClient.generate_response(Personas.AI_COACH['system_prompt'], f_p, [], st.session_state['engine_config'], resume_context=st.session_state['resume_text'])\n                        \n                        # Save session with dominant tone\n                        HistoryManager.save_session(avg_w, t_f, dom_tone, \"Multi-Turn\")\n                        \n                        st.session_state.update({\n                            'final_feedback': f_f, \n                            'full_transcript': full_t, \n                            'avg_wpm': avg_w, \n                            'total_fillers': t_f, \n                            'total_duration': t_d,\n                            'total_pauses': t_p,\n                            'total_blunders': t_b,\n                            'dominant_tone': dom_tone\n                        })\n                render_final_analysis(st.session_state)\n\n        with tab_history: render_history_dashboard()\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        st.error(f\"🚨 Error: {e}\"); st.code(traceback.format_exc())\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()",
    "ai_proposed_content": "import os\nimport sys\nimport streamlit as st\nimport json\nimport ast\nimport time\nimport traceback\nimport base64\nimport re\nimport requests\nfrom dotenv import load_dotenv\n\n# Load environment variables from .env file\nload_dotenv()\n\n# --- PATH INJECTION ---\nroot_dir = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))\nif root_dir not in sys.path: sys.path.insert(0, root_dir)\nsrc_dir = os.path.join(root_dir, \"src\")\nif src_dir not in sys.path: sys.path.insert(0, src_dir)\n\n# --- INTERNAL IMPORTS ---\nfrom src.utils.diagnostics import get_logger, log_system_info\nfrom src.api.client import APIClient\nfrom src.ui.recorder import record_audio\nfrom src.utils.file_manager import FileManager\nfrom src.utils.history import HistoryManager\nfrom src.backend.hardware import HardwareInfo\nfrom src.backend.personas import Personas\nfrom src.utils.text_processor import clean_llm_text, parse_file\nfrom src.ui.dashboard import render_final_analysis, render_history_dashboard\n\n# --- INITIALIZATION ---\nif 'sys_logged' not in st.session_state:\n    log_system_info()\n    st.session_state['sys_logged'] = True\n\nlogger = get_logger()\nFileManager.initialize_directories()\n\n# --- CACHED WRAPPERS ---\n@st.cache_data(ttl=300)\ndef get_cached_models():\n    return APIClient.get_local_models()\n\n# --- UI COMPONENTS ---\n\n@st.fragment(run_every=2)\ndef render_resource_usage():\n    \"\"\"Isolated fragment for high-frequency resource monitoring.\"\"\"\n    hw_status = APIClient.get_hardware_status()\n    st.subheader(\"🖥️ Resource Usage\")\n    if hw_status:\n        stats = hw_status.get(\"stats\", {})\n        c_v, r_p = stats.get('cpu_percent', 0), stats.get('ram_percent', 0)\n        r_u, r_t = stats.get('ram_used_gb', 0), stats.get('ram_total_gb', 0)\n        st.progress(min(max(float(c_v) / 100.0, 0.0), 1.0), text=f\"CPU: {c_v}%\")\n        st.progress(min(max(float(r_p) / 100.0, 0.0), 1.0), text=f\"RAM: {r_u}/{r_t} GB\")\n        if stats.get(\"gpu_detected\"):\n            v_p, v_u, v_t = stats.get('vram_percent', 0), stats.get('vram_used_gb', 0), stats.get('vram_total_gb', 0)\n            st.progress(min(max(float(v_p) / 100.0, 0.0), 1.0), text=f\"GPU VRAM: {v_u}/{v_t} GB\")\n    else:\n        st.error(\"🔴 Backend: Offline\")\n\n@st.fragment(run_every=5)\ndef render_api_panel(w_n):\n    \"\"\"Dedicated section for AI Engine selection and live connection status.\"\"\"\n    st.subheader(\"🔌 API & Inference\")\n    \n    providers = [\"Ollama (Local)\", \"OpenAI\", \"Google Gemini\", \"Anthropic\"]\n    default_provider = st.session_state.get('default_provider', \"Google Gemini\")\n    default_idx = providers.index(default_provider)\n    provider = st.selectbox(\"Inference Provider\", providers, index=default_idx, key=f\"prov_sel_{w_n}\")\n    \n    if provider == \"Ollama (Local)\":\n        models = get_cached_models()\n        selected_model = st.selectbox(\"Local Model\", models, key=f\"model_sel_{w_n}\")\n        with st.expander(\"⬇️ Download Model\"):\n            tag = st.selectbox(\"Tag\", [\"llama3.1:8b\", \"gemma2:9b\", \"mistral:7b\", \"-- Other --\"], key=f\"pull_tag_{w_n}\")\n            if tag == \"-- Other --\": tag = st.text_input(\"Custom Tag\", key=f\"pull_custom_{w_n}\")\n            if st.button(\"Pull Model\", use_container_width=True):\n                p = st.progress(0); res = APIClient.pull_model_stream(tag)\n                for line in res.iter_lines():\n                    if line:\n                        d = json.loads(line.decode('utf-8'))\n                        if \"completed\" in d and \"total\" in d and d[\"total\"] > 0: p.progress(d[\"completed\"]/d[\"total\"])\n                st.success(\"Ready!\"); st.cache_data.clear(); st.rerun()\n        api_key = None\n    else:\n        m_map = {\"OpenAI\": [\"gpt-5.4-nano\", \"gpt-5-nano\", \"o4-mini\", \"gpt-4o-mini\"], \"Anthropic\": [\"claude-sonnet-4-6\",\"claude-haiku-4-5\", \"claude-sonnet-4-5\", \"claude-sonnet-4-0\"], \"Google Gemini\": [\"gemini-3-flash-preview\", \"gemini-3.1-flash-lite-preview\", \"gemini-2.5-flash-lite\"]}\n        selected_model = st.selectbox(\"Model\", m_map.get(provider, [\"-- Other --\"]) + [\"-- Other --\"], key=f\"ext_model_{w_n}\")\n        if selected_model == \"-- Other --\": selected_model = st.text_input(\"Custom Model\", key=f\"ext_custom_{w_n}\")\n        \n        key_links = {\"OpenAI\": \"https://platform.openai.com/api-keys\", \"Anthropic\": \"https://platform.claude.com/settings/keys\", \"Google Gemini\": \"https://aistudio.google.com/app/api-keys\"}\n        st.caption(f\"ℹ️ [Get {provider} Key]({key_links.get(provider, '#')})\")\n\n        # UNIQUE KEY PER PROVIDER: This ensures fields are isolated and don't mix up keys\n        api_key_key = f\"api_key_{provider}_{w_n}\"\n        api_key = st.text_input(\"Secret API Key\", value=st.session_state['saved_keys'].get(provider, \"\"), type=\"password\", key=api_key_key)\n\n        if api_key != st.session_state['saved_keys'].get(provider):\n            st.session_state['saved_keys'][provider] = api_key\n            FileManager.save_keys(st.session_state['saved_keys'])\n    # Sync Config\n    compute_target = st.session_state.get(f\"comp_target_{w_n}\", \"CPU & RAM Core\")\n    st.session_state['engine_config'] = {\"provider\": provider, \"model\": selected_model, \"compute\": compute_target, \"api_key\": api_key}\n\n    # Connection Status Badges\n    st.write(\"\")\n    hw_status = APIClient.get_hardware_status()\n    if hw_status:\n        st.success(\"🟢 Backend: Online\")\n        success, msg = APIClient.test_connection(st.session_state['engine_config'])\n        if success: st.success(\"🟢 AI Engine: Ready\")\n        else: st.error(msg)\n    else:\n        st.error(\"🔴 Backend: Offline\")\n\n@st.fragment\ndef render_config_panel(w_n):\n    \"\"\"Isolated fragment for hardware and voice configuration.\"\"\"\n    st.subheader(\"⚙️ Configuration\")\n    \n    # 1. Hardware Detection & Selection\n    hw = HardwareInfo()\n    rec_mode, rec_text = hw.get_recommendation()\n    \n    # Detect and show physical hardware names\n    hw_status = APIClient.get_hardware_status()\n    det_hw = hw_status.get(\"detected_hw\", \"Standard CPU\") if hw_status else \"Scanning...\"\n    \n    c_options = [\"NVIDIA GPU\", \"CPU & RAM Core\"]\n    if hw.is_apple_silicon:\n        c_options = [\"Apple Silicon\", \"CPU & RAM Core\"]\n    \n    compute_target = st.radio(\"Compute Allocation\", c_options, horizontal=True, key=f\"comp_target_{w_n}\")\n    \n    # Show ALL detected hardware\n    st.caption(f\"**Detected Hardware:** {det_hw}\")\n    if hw.has_nvidia and hw.is_apple_silicon: # Rare edge case\n        st.caption(\"🔍 Multiple accelerators found (NVIDIA + M-Series)\")\n    \n    with st.expander(\"💡 Hardware Helper\"):\n        st.info(rec_text)\n\n    # 2. Voice Selection\n    v_opt = st.radio(\"Coach Voice\", [\"Male\", \"Female\"], horizontal=True, key=f\"v_opt_{w_n}\")\n    st.session_state['selected_voice'] = \"en-US-GuyNeural\" if v_opt == \"Male\" else \"en-US-AvaNeural\"\n\n    # 3. Active Mode Logic (Shows real-time backend usage)\n    if hw_status:\n        actual_using = \"Standard CPU (Int8)\"\n        if compute_target == \"NVIDIA GPU\" and hw_status.get(\"has_nvidia\"):\n            actual_using = \"NVIDIA GPU (FP16)\"\n        elif compute_target == \"Apple Silicon\" and hw_status.get(\"is_apple_silicon\"):\n            actual_using = \"Apple Neural Engine (FP32)\"\n        st.info(f\"**Active Mode:** {actual_using}\")\n\ndef trigger_voice(text):\n    \"\"\"Fetches audio and sets play state.\"\"\"\n    if not text: return\n    clean_text = clean_llm_text(text)\n    voice = st.session_state.get('selected_voice', \"en-US-GuyNeural\")\n    audio_bytes = APIClient.generate_speech(clean_text, voice=voice)\n    if audio_bytes:\n        st.session_state['play_now_bytes'] = audio_bytes\n        st.session_state['audio_nonce'] = st.session_state.get('audio_nonce', 0) + 1\n        return True\n    return False\n\n@st.fragment\ndef render_interview_loop(info):\n    \"\"\"Isolated interview loop for real-time interaction without page flickering.\"\"\"\n    st.subheader(\"🎙️ Live Interview Simulator\")\n    st.info(f\"⏱️ **Stage:** {info['meaning']} | **Interviewer:** {info['persona']}\")\n    \n    for idx, msg in enumerate(st.session_state['chat_history']):\n        with st.chat_message(msg[\"role\"], avatar=(\"🤖\" if msg[\"role\"] == \"assistant\" else \"👤\")):\n            st.write(msg[\"content\"])\n            if msg[\"role\"] == \"assistant\":\n                if st.button(\"🔊 Replay\", key=f\"replay_{idx}\"):\n                    trigger_voice(msg[\"content\"]); st.rerun()\n\n    st.divider()\n    if 'rec_nonce' not in st.session_state: st.session_state['rec_nonce'] = 0\n    audio_path = record_audio(key=f\"recorder_{st.session_state['rec_nonce']}\")\n    \n    c_sub, c_end = st.columns(2)\n    compute_type = st.session_state.get('engine_config', {}).get('compute', 'CPU & RAM Core')\n    \n    if audio_path and c_sub.button(\"🗣️ Submit Answer\", type=\"primary\", use_container_width=True):\n        with st.spinner(\"Processing...\"):\n            t, m, d, e = APIClient.process_audio(audio_path, info['recommended_mode'], compute_type)\n            if not e:\n                st.session_state['aggregated_metrics'].append({\"transcript\": t, \"metrics\": m, \"duration\": d})\n                st.session_state['chat_history'].append({\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": t})\n                with st.spinner(\"Thinking...\"):\n                    nxt = APIClient.generate_response(st.session_state['sys_p'], t, st.session_state['chat_history'][:-1], st.session_state['engine_config'], resume_context=st.session_state.get('resume_text', \"\"), job_context=st.session_state.get('job_desc_text', \"\"))\n                    trigger_voice(nxt)\n                    st.session_state['chat_history'].append({\"role\": \"assistant\", \"content\": clean_llm_text(nxt)})\n                    st.session_state['rec_nonce'] += 1\n                    st.rerun()\n\n    if c_end.button(\"🛑 End Interview & Analyze\", use_container_width=True):\n        if audio_path:\n            with st.spinner(\"Finalizing...\"):\n                t, m, d, e = APIClient.process_audio(audio_path, info['recommended_mode'], compute_type)\n                if not e:\n                    st.session_state['aggregated_metrics'].append({\"transcript\": t, \"metrics\": m, \"duration\": d})\n                    st.session_state['chat_history'].append({\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": t})\n        st.session_state['interview_complete'] = True; st.rerun()\n\n@st.fragment\ndef render_setup_wizard(w_n):\n    \"\"\"Isolated setup wizard for role definition and document processing.\"\"\"\n    with st.expander(\"🛠️ Interview Setup Wizard\", expanded=(st.session_state['setup_step'] < 3)):\n        st.markdown(\"### 1. Define Target Role\")\n        c1, c2, c3 = st.columns(3)\n        ind = c1.text_input(\"Industry\", placeholder=\"e.g., Tech\", key=f\"ind_in_{w_n}\")\n        role = c2.text_input(\"Job Title\", placeholder=\"e.g., Backend Developer\", key=f\"role_in_{w_n}\")\n        sen = c3.selectbox(\"Seniority\", [\"Entry-Level\", \"Mid-Level\", \"Senior / Lead\", \"Executive\"], key=f\"sen_in_{w_n}\")\n        \n        st.divider()\n        st.markdown(\"### 2. Contextual Data (Optional)\")\n        c_res, c_jd = st.columns(2)\n        res_f = c_res.file_uploader(\"Upload Resume\", type=[\"pdf\", \"txt\", \"docx\"], key=f\"res_up_{w_n}\")\n        jd_f = c_jd.file_uploader(\"Upload Job Description\", type=[\"pdf\", \"txt\", \"docx\"], key=f\"jd_up_{w_n}\")\n        \n        if res_f:\n            res_text = parse_file(res_f)\n            if res_text: st.session_state['resume_text'] = res_text\n            else: st.warning(f\"⚠️ Could not read resume: {res_f.name}\"); st.session_state['resume_text'] = None\n        else: st.session_state['resume_text'] = \"\"\n\n        if jd_f:\n            jd_text = parse_file(jd_f)\n            if jd_text: st.session_state['job_desc_text'] = jd_text\n            else: st.warning(f\"⚠️ Could not read job description: {jd_f.name}\"); st.session_state['job_desc_text'] = None\n        else: st.session_state['job_desc_text'] = \"\"\n\n        if st.button(\"Generate Interview Rounds\", disabled=not (ind and role), use_container_width=True):\n            hw_status = APIClient.get_hardware_status()\n            if not hw_status: st.error(\"🔴 Backend Offline\")\n            else:\n                success, msg = APIClient.test_connection(st.session_state['engine_config'])\n                if not success: st.error(f\"❌ Connection Error: {msg}\")\n                else:\n                    with st.spinner(\"Structuring...\"):\n                        p = (f\"List 4 unique interview rounds for a {sen} {role} in the {ind} industry. Output ONLY a Python list of strings.\")\n                        resp = APIClient.generate_response(\"Expert recruiter. Return ONLY a Python list.\", p, [], st.session_state['engine_config'])\n                        try:\n                            import re; m = re.search(r\"\\[.*\\]\", resp, re.DOTALL)\n                            rounds = ast.literal_eval(m.group()) if m else [resp]\n                            st.session_state['rounds'] = [str(r).strip() for r in rounds if len(str(r)) < 150]\n                        except: st.session_state['rounds'] = [\"1. Initial Screen\", \"2. Technical Round\", \"3. Culture Fit\", \"4. Final Manager\"]\n                        st.session_state['setup_step'] = 2; st.rerun()\n\n        if st.session_state['setup_step'] >= 2:\n            st.divider()\n            st.markdown(\"### 3. Select Stage & Persona\")\n            sel_r = st.selectbox(\"Target Interview Round:\", st.session_state['rounds'], key=f\"round_sel_{w_n}\")\n            sel_p = st.selectbox(\"Interviewer Style:\", list(Personas.PERSONA_PROMPTS.keys()), key=f\"persona_sel_{w_n}\")\n            if st.button(\"Start Interview Simulation\", type=\"primary\", use_container_width=True):\n                if not sel_r: st.error(\"Please generate interview rounds in Step 1 first!\")\n                else:\n                    with st.spinner(\"Writing questions...\"):\n                        st.session_state.update({'selected_persona_label': sel_p, 'selected_round': sel_r, 'seniority': sen, 'job_title': role, 'industry': ind})\n                        persona_cfg = Personas.get_interviewer_by_type(sel_r.split(\" \")[0], sen)\n                        st.session_state['round_info'] = {\"meaning\": persona_cfg['meaning'], \"persona\": persona_cfg['persona'], \"recommended_mode\": \"Balanced\"}\n                        q_resp = APIClient.generate_questions(sen, role, ind, sel_r, st.session_state['engine_config'], st.session_state['resume_text'], st.session_state['job_desc_text'])\n                        raw_qs = [q.strip() for q in q_resp.split('\\n') if len(q.strip()) > 10]\n                        st.session_state['custom_questions'] = [clean_llm_text(q) for q in raw_qs]\n                        st.session_state['setup_step'] = 3; st.rerun()\n\n# --- MAIN APP ---\ndef main():\n    try:\n        if 'wipe_nonce' not in st.session_state: st.session_state['wipe_nonce'] = 0\n        w_n = st.session_state['wipe_nonce']\n\n        if 'default_provider' not in st.session_state:\n            try:\n                res = requests.get(\"http://127.0.0.1:11434/api/tags\", timeout=1)\n                if res.status_code == 200: st.session_state['default_provider'] = \"Ollama (Local)\"\n                else: st.session_state['default_provider'] = \"Google Gemini\"\n            except: st.session_state['default_provider'] = \"Google Gemini\"\n\n        if 'saved_keys' not in st.session_state:\n            st.session_state['saved_keys'] = FileManager.load_saved_keys()\n\n        # --- SIDEBAR ---\n        with st.sidebar:\n            st.image(\"assets/Data-Drifters.png\", width=\"stretch\")\n            \n            # 1. Resource Usage (Top)\n            render_resource_usage()\n            st.divider()\n            \n            # 2. API & Inference (Middle)\n            render_api_panel(w_n)\n            st.divider()\n            \n            # 3. Configuration (Bottom - Now a Fragment)\n            render_config_panel(w_n)\n            \n            st.divider()\n            if st.button(\"🔄 Start New Interview\", use_container_width=True):\n                new_nonce = st.session_state.get('wipe_nonce', 0) + 1\n                keep = ['engine_config', 'sys_logged', 'selected_voice', 'saved_keys', 'default_provider']\n                for k in list(st.session_state.keys()):\n                    if k not in keep: del st.session_state[k]\n                st.session_state['wipe_nonce'] = new_nonce; st.rerun()\n\n            with st.expander(\"🗑️ Danger Zone\"):\n                if st.button(\"Delete All Data\", type=\"primary\", use_container_width=True):\n                    FileManager.cleanup_all_data(); st.rerun()\n\n        st.title(\"🎙️ AI Interview Coach\")\n\n        if st.session_state.get('play_now_bytes'):\n            b64 = base64.b64encode(st.session_state['play_now_bytes']).decode()\n            n = st.session_state.get('audio_nonce', 0)\n            st.components.v1.html(f'<audio id=\"a_{n}\" autoplay><source src=\"data:audio/mp3;base64,{b64}\" type=\"audio/mp3\"></audio><script>document.getElementById(\"a_{n}\").play();</script>', height=1)\n            st.session_state['play_now_bytes'] = None\n\n        tab_coach, tab_history = st.tabs([\"🎯 Live Coach\", \"📈 Session History\"])\n        \n        with tab_coach:\n            if 'setup_step' not in st.session_state: st.session_state['setup_step'] = 1\n            if 'rounds' not in st.session_state: st.session_state['rounds'] = []\n            if 'aggregated_metrics' not in st.session_state: st.session_state['aggregated_metrics'] = []\n            \n            if st.session_state['setup_step'] < 3: render_setup_wizard(w_n)\n\n            if st.session_state['setup_step'] == 3 and not st.session_state.get('interview_complete'):\n                # (Logic handled in fragments)\n                pass\n\n            if st.session_state['setup_step'] == 3 and not st.session_state.get('interview_complete'):\n                if 'chat_history' not in st.session_state:\n                    st.session_state['sys_p'] = Personas.get_interview_sys_prompt(st.session_state['selected_persona_label'], st.session_state['selected_round'], st.session_state['seniority'], st.session_state['job_title'], st.session_state['industry'], st.session_state['resume_text'], st.session_state['job_desc_text'])\n                    st.session_state['chat_history'] = []\n                    with st.spinner(\"🎙️ Coach is entering the room...\"):\n                        first_q = APIClient.generate_response(st.session_state['sys_p'], \"Start the interview. Greet me and ask ONLY your first question.\", [], st.session_state['engine_config'], resume_context=st.session_state.get('resume_text', \"\"), job_context=st.session_state.get('job_desc_text', \"\"))\n                        first_q = clean_llm_text(first_q); trigger_voice(first_q)\n                        st.session_state['chat_history'].append({\"role\": \"assistant\", \"content\": first_q}); st.rerun()\n                render_interview_loop(st.session_state['round_info'])\n\n            if st.session_state.get('interview_complete'):\n                if 'final_feedback' not in st.session_state:\n                    with st.spinner(\"Analyzing...\"):\n                        v = len(st.session_state['aggregated_metrics'])\n                        t_w = sum([m['metrics']['wpm'] for m in st.session_state['aggregated_metrics']])\n                        t_f = sum([m['metrics']['filler_count'] for m in st.session_state['aggregated_metrics']])\n                        t_d = sum([m['duration'] for m in st.session_state['aggregated_metrics']])\n                        \n                        # New Metrics Aggregation\n                        t_p = sum([m['metrics'].get('pause_count', 0) for m in st.session_state['aggregated_metrics']])\n                        t_b = sum([m['metrics'].get('blunder_count', 0) for m in st.session_state['aggregated_metrics']])\n                        tones = [m['metrics'].get('tone_label', 'Neutral') for m in st.session_state['aggregated_metrics']]\n                        dom_tone = max(set(tones), key=tones.count) if tones else \"Neutral\"\n                        \n                        avg_w = t_w / v if v > 0 else 0; full_t = \"\"\n                        for msg in st.session_state['chat_history']: full_t += f\"<b>{'Interviewer' if msg['role'] == 'assistant' else 'Candidate'}:</b> {msg['content']}<br><br>\\n\"\n                        f_p = Personas.get_final_feedback_prompt(st.session_state['seniority'], st.session_state['job_title'], st.session_state['industry'], full_t)\n                        f_f = APIClient.generate_response(Personas.AI_COACH['system_prompt'], f_p, [], st.session_state['engine_config'], resume_context=st.session_state['resume_text'])\n                        \n                        # Save session with dominant tone\n                        HistoryManager.save_session(avg_w, t_f, dom_tone, \"Multi-Turn\")\n                        \n                        st.session_state.update({\n                            'final_feedback': f_f, \n                            'full_transcript': full_t, \n                            'avg_wpm': avg_w, \n                            'total_fillers': t_f, \n                            'total_duration': t_d,\n                            'total_pauses': t_p,\n                            'total_blunders': t_b,\n                            'dominant_tone': dom_tone\n                        })\n                render_final_analysis(st.session_state)\n\n        with tab_history: render_history_dashboard()\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        st.error(f\"🚨 Error: {e}\"); st.code(traceback.format_exc())\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()."
  }
}

Strip all leading/trailing brackets, quotes, and whitespace iteratively\n    while raw and (raw[0] in '[\"\\' \\n\\r\\t' or raw[-1] in ']\"\\' \\n\\r\\t'):\n        raw = raw.strip('[]\"\\' \\n\\r\\t')\n    \n    # 2. Remove specific prefixes\n    raw = re.sub(r'^(Question\\s*\\d+:)\\s*', '', raw, flags=re.IGNORECASE)\n    \n    return raw.strip()\n\ndef parse_file(uploaded_file):\n    \"\"\"Extracts text from PDF, TXT, or DOCX files.\"\"\"\n    if not uploaded_file: return None\n    \n    try:\n        # 1. Text Files\n        if uploaded_file.type == \"text/plain\":\n            return str(uploaded_file.read(), \"utf-8\")\n            \n        # 2. PDF Files (using pdfplumber)\n        elif uploaded_file.type == \"application/pdf\":\n            import pdfplumber\n            text = \"\"\n            with pdfplumber.open(uploaded_file) as pdf:\n                for page in pdf.pages:\n                    page_text = page.extract_text()\n                    if page_text:\n                        text += page_text + \"\\n\"\n            return text.strip() if text.strip() else None\n            \n        # 3. {

    "file_path": "CONTEXT.md",
    "content": "# 📄 Project Context: AI-Powered Interview Coaching System (MVP)\n\nThis document serves as the comprehensive source of truth for the project's architecture, capabilities, and technical innovations, intended for use in final documentation and presentations.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Project Identity & Vision\n*   **Goal**: To democratize elite professional coaching by providing a private, secure, and data-driven interview simulation environment.\n*   **Core Value Proposition**: Combines advanced **Acoustic Analysis** (how you speak) with **Semantic LLM Intelligence** (what you say) to provide feedback that previously required expensive human coaching.\n*   **Privacy Mandate**: Designed for \"Local-First\" execution using Ollama and Faster-Whisper, ensuring sensitive resumes and audio recordings never leave the user's machine.\n\n---\n\n## 2. Technical Architecture\nThe system uses a **Decoupled Client-Server** model to isolate heavy AI processing from the user interface.\n\n### 2.1 Backend: The AI Engine (FastAPI)\n*   **Role**: Handles transcription, acoustic scoring, LLM routing, and hardware telemetry.\n*   **Language**: Python 3.11 - 3.13.\n*   **Web Framework**: FastAPI (Uvicorn).\n*   **Security**: Secured via a custom **X-Internal-Key** authentication middleware.\n\n### 2.2 Frontend: The Command Center (Streamlit)\n*   **Role**: Manages the Interview Setup Wizard, real-time chat, audio recording, and visual analytics.\n*   **Visuals**: Uses Plotly for interactive trend charts and custom HTML/JS injection for reliable browser-based audio autoplay.\n*   **Optimization**: Implements **Streamlit Fragments** to isolate high-frequency resource monitoring from the main conversational loop.\n\n---\n\n## 3. The \"AI Ears\": Acoustic & Linguistic Pipeline\n\n### 3.1 Real-Time Transcription\n*   **Library**: `faster-whisper` (CTranslate2).\n*   **Hardware Awareness**: \n    *   **NVIDIA GPU**: Uses CUDA with `medium.en` model (FP16).\n    *   **Apple Silicon**: Optimizes for the Neural Engine using `medium.en` (FP32).\n    *   **Standard CPU**: Falls back to `small.en` or `tiny.en` using Int8 quantization.\n*   **Efficiency**: Implements a **Model Cache Singleton** to ensure near-zero latency after the initial load.\n\n### 3.2 Deep Acoustic Scoring\n*   **Metrics Extracted**:\n    *   **Pacing**: Words Per Minute (WPM) calculated via transcript length vs. precise audio duration.\n    *   **Fluency**: Detects filler words (\"um\", \"uh\", \"like\"), stutters (hyphenated words), and repetitions using Regex.\n    *   **Awkward Pauses**: Identifies silences > 1.5 seconds using `librosa` energy thresholds.\n    *   **Tone Analysis**: Classifies the candidate's vibe (e.g., Confident, Nervous, Energetic) based on **Pitch Variation (YIN Algorithm)** and **RMS Energy**.\n\n---\n\n## 4. The \"AI Brain\": Multi-Engine LLM Support\n*   **Local Engine**: Full integration with **Ollama** for 100% private, offline coaching.\n*   **Cloud Frontier**: Support for **OpenAI** (o1/o4-mini ready), **Google Gemini** (v1beta Flash/Pro), and **Anthropic Claude**.\n*   **Personalization**: \n    *   **Context Upload**: Parses `.pdf` and `.docx` files to inject Resume and Job Description data into the AI's memory.\n    *   **Dynamic Greetings**: AI identifies the candidate's name from the resume and greets them personally.\n    *   **Persona Impersonation**: If a recruiter's name is found in the Job Description, the AI adopts that specific persona.\n\n---\n\n## 5. User Experience & Gamification\n*   **Interview Setup Wizard**: A 3-step process to define roles, upload context, and select from adaptive interviewers (Friendly HR, Tech Lead, Stress Interviewer).\n*   **Performance Badges**: A gamified system rewarding progress (e.g., \"Golden Pacer\" for ideal WPM, \"Silver Tongue\" for low fillers).\n*   **Enterprise Reporting**: Generates a professional **PDF Performance Report** using the STAR framework, containing metrics, feedback, and full transcripts.\n\n---\n\n## 6. Deployment & Dev-Ops Innovation\n*   **Universal Setup**: One-click automation scripts (`start.bat`, `start.sh`, `install.py`).\n*   **Dependency Intelligence**: Automatically detects OS and Python versions to install the \"Sweet Version\" of PyTorch (handling Nightly CUDA 12.4 for Python 3.13 on Windows).\n*   **Portable Drivers**: Automatically downloads and configures a portable version of **FFmpeg** on Windows to remove manual path-setup hurdles.\n*   **Hybrid Ready**: Configured for local backend usage via **ngrok**, allowing the frontend to be hosted on the public cloud (Streamlit Cloud) while maintaining local data privacy.\n\n---\n**Status**: MVP Complete and Verified.\n**Copyright**: Karanveer Singh, Amel Korandippillil Sunil, Orlando Santiago Cardenas Vargas.\n",

### 1. Update `CONTEXT.md` with Deep Technical Details

```json
{
  "name": "write_file",
  "args": {
    "file_path": "CONTEXT.md",
    "content": "# 📄 Deep Technical Context: AI Interview Coaching System (MVP)\n\nThis document provides an exhaustive technical and functional breakdown of the \"Data Drifters\" AI Interview Coach. It is designed to serve as the primary source of truth for final project reports and MVP presentations.\n\n---\n\n## 1. System Architecture: The Decoupled Hybrid Model\nThe system is built on a **Modular Client-Server Architecture** to ensure high-performance AI processing without compromising the responsiveness of the User Interface.\n\n### 1.1 Backend: The AI Intelligence Hub (FastAPI)\n*   **Infrastructure**: Python 3.11+ running a **FastAPI** server served by **Uvicorn**.\n*   **Concurrency**: All heavy computational routes (transcription, scoring, LLM calls) are implemented as synchronous `def` functions. This allows FastAPI to automatically manage them in an external **threadpool**, preventing the server's event loop from blocking during multi-gigabyte model loads.\n*   **Security (Inter-Service)**: Implements an **X-Internal-Key** authentication layer. Every request from the frontend must carry a secret key (synced via `.env`) to prevent unauthorized access to the local AI engines.\n*   **Cross-Origin Support**: Fully configured with **CORS Middleware** to allow the frontend (hosted on Streamlit Cloud) to securely communicate with the local backend via **ngrok**.\n\n### 1.2 Frontend: The Reactive Command Center (Streamlit)\n*   **Heartbeat Fragment**: Uses `@st.fragment` to run a \"Unified Status Monitor.\" This background loop updates hardware stats and connection badges every 5 seconds without triggering a full page refresh (preventing UI flicker).\n*   **State Management**: Utilizes `st.session_state` to track a complex 3-step Interview Setup Wizard, preserving API keys, extracted resume text, and multi-turn chat history.\n*   **Audio Autoplay Implementation**: Solves the browser \"autoplay block\" by injecting a hidden HTML `<audio>` component. It uses a **Nonce-based unique key** system to force the browser to treat every new coach response as a fresh interaction, ensuring reliable voice output.\n\n---\n\n## 2. The \"AI Ears\": High-Fidelity Acoustic Pipeline\n\n### 2.1 Hardware-Aware Transcription (`AudioProcessor`)\n*   **Engine**: `faster-whisper` utilizing the CTranslate2 C++ backend.\n*   **Precision Intelligence**:\n    *   **NVIDIA GPU**: Automatically detects CUDA and loads the `medium.en` model using **Int8_Float16** quantization for maximum speed.\n    *   **Apple Silicon**: Detects M-series chips and optimizes for the **Apple Neural Engine (MPS)** using `float32` precision.\n    *   **Standard CPU**: Falls back to `small.en` with **Int8** quantization to maintain accuracy on lower-end hardware.\n*   **VRAM Management**: Implements a **Singleton Model Cache**. When switching between GPU and CPU modes, the system explicitly clears the VRAM cache and runs Python's Garbage Collector (`gc.collect()`) to prevent Out-of-Memory (OOM) crashes.\n\n### 2.2 Deep Acoustic Scoring (`AcousticScorer`)\n*   **Signal Processing**: Leverages `librosa` to analyze raw PCM audio data.\n*   **Pitch Analysis**: Uses the **YIN Algorithm** for fundamental frequency (F0) tracking. This allows the system to measure \"Pitch Variation\"—a key indicator of confidence vs. nervousness.\n*   **Energy Analysis**: Calculates **Root Mean Square (RMS)** energy to determine vocal volume and intensity.\n*   **Fluency Engine (Regex)**:\n    *   **Fillers**: Detects `um`, `uh`, `ah`, `hmm`, `like`, `you know`, etc.\n    *   **Stutters**: Uses word-boundary regex (`\\b(\\w+)-\\1\\b`) to catch hyphenated repetitions.\n    *   **Pauses**: Identifies silences > 1.5 seconds using decibel-threshold splitting.\n\n---\n\n## 3. The \"AI Brain\": Multi-Frontier LLM Integration\n\n### 3.1 Unified LLM Client\n*   **Local Privacy**: Directly orchestrates **Ollama** via its REST API, allowing users to run `Llama 3`, `Gemma`, or `Mistral` with 0% data leakage.\n*   **Cloud Frontier**: Integrated support for **OpenAI (GPT-4o)**, **Anthropic (Claude 3.5)**, and **Google Gemini (2.0 Flash)**.\n*   **Reasoning Model Handling**: Specifically handles **OpenAI O-Series** (`o1`, `o4-mini`) by:\n    *   Automatically swapping `max_tokens` for `max_completion_tokens`.\n    *   Injecting system instructions into the `user` role (as these models lack a traditional `system` role).\n\n### 3.2 Context-Aware Personalization\n*   **Document Parsing**: Uses `pypdf` and `pdfplumber` for high-fidelity PDF extraction and `python-docx` for Word files.\n*   **Information Extraction**: The AI scans extracted text to:\n    *   Identify the candidate's name for a personalized greeting.\n    *   Extract the recruiter's name from the Job Description to impersonate the hiring manager.\n    *   Cross-reference answers against resume claims to look for inconsistencies.\n\n---\n\n## 4. Reporting & Gamification\n\n### 4.1 STAR Framework Evaluation\nThe final feedback logic forces the AI to evaluate responses based on the **Situation, Task, Action, and Result** (STAR) framework, providing professional-grade career coaching.\n\n### 4.2 Gamified progression\n*   **Performance Badges**: A logic-driven achievement system (e.g., \"Golden Pacer\" for 130-160 WPM, \"Silver Tongue\" for <2 fillers per turn).\n*   **Trend Analytics**: Uses **Plotly Express** to render interactive time-series charts showing the user's improvement across multiple sessions.\n\n### 4.3 Enterprise PDF Generation\n*   **Engine**: `fpdf2` with HTML-to-PDF conversion.\n*   **Sanitization**: Implements custom **Unicode Cleaning** to convert problematic smart-quotes and emojis into PDF-safe characters, preventing document corruption.\n\n---\n\n## 5. Deployment & Automation Innovation\n\n### 5.1 One-Click Setup (`install.py`)\n*   **Intelligent Installer**: A comprehensive script that:\n    1.  Scans hardware (CPU threads, RAM, GPU Vendor).\n    2.  Creates a clean virtual environment.\n    3.  Selects the **\"Sweet Version\"** of PyTorch (handling the specialized Nightly CUDA 12.4 builds for Python 3.13 on Windows).\n    4.  Downloads and configures **Portable FFmpeg** automatically.\n\n### 5.2 Universal Launchers\n*   `start.bat` (Windows) and `start.sh` (Mac/Linux) provide a \"double-click\" experience for users, handling both the backend and frontend orchestration simultaneously.\n\n---\n**MVP Status**: Fully Verified & Documented.\n**Data Drifters Team**: Karanveer Singh, Amel Korandippillil Sunil, Orlando Santiago Cardenas Vargas.\n",

    "content": "# 📄 Exhaustive Technical Specification: AI Interview Coaching System (MVP)\n\nThis document provides the most granular level of detail for the system's architecture, AI pipelines, and implementation logic. It is the definitive source for final project reports, engineering documentation, and MVP presentations.\n\n---\n\n## 1. System Architecture & Infrastructure\n\n### 1.1 Multi-Layer Security Model\n*   **Inter-Service Auth**: Every API request is intercepted by a `verify_internal_key` dependency.\n    *   **Header**: `X-Internal-Key`\n    *   **Enforcement**: 401 Unauthorized for mismatches; 500 Server Error if the key is missing from the host environment.\n*   **Environment Orchestration**: Uses `python-dotenv` to bridge local `.env` files with OS-level environment variables, ensuring that security keys never leak into the source code or frontend logs.\n\n### 1.2 Concurrency & Event-Loop Optimization\n*   **FastAPI Execution Model**: Routes are defined as standard `def` (synchronous) functions. This is a deliberate choice:\n    *   **Reasoning**: ML tasks (transcription/scoring) are CPU/GPU-bound, not I/O-bound. Running them in `async def` would block the entire server's event loop. \n    *   **Solution**: FastAPI automatically runs these `def` functions in an external **ThreadPoolExecutor**, keeping the server responsive to telemetry heartbeats even during heavy 30-second AI inferences.\n\n---\n\n## 2. The \"AI Ears\": Deep Acoustic Analysis Engine\n\n### 2.1 The Transcription Pipeline (`AudioProcessor`)\n*   **Engine**: `faster-whisper` (based on OpenAI's Whisper but rewritten in C++ for 4x speed).\n*   **Configuration Parameters**:\n    *   **Beam Size**: 5 (balance between speed and accuracy).\n    *   **Initial Prompt**: *\"Umm, I-I think... well, actually... so your...\"* (This biases the AI to recognize and transcribe natural filler words instead of \"cleaning\" them away).\n*   **Hardware-Aware Loading Logic**:\n    *   **NVIDIA**: `cuda` device | `medium.en` model | `int8_float16` quantization.\n    *   **Apple M-Series**: `cpu` device | `medium.en` model | `float32` (uses AMX/Neural Engine via Accelerate framework).\n    *   **Legacy CPU**: `cpu` device | `small.en` or `tiny.en` | `int8` (optimized for systems with < 12GB RAM).\n\n### 2.2 The Scoring Logic (`AcousticScorer`)\n*   **Signal Processing**: Uses `librosa` at a **16kHz sample rate** (optimized for human speech).\n*   **Fundamental Frequency (F0) Tracking**: Uses the **YIN Algorithm** rather than standard auto-correlation for higher stability against background noise.\n*   **Threshold-Based Fluency Detection**:\n    *   **Filler Word Regex**: `\\b(um+?|uh+?|ah+?|hmm+|like|you know|sort of|kind of|i mean|basically|actually)\\b`\n    *   **Stutter Regex**: `\\b(\\w+)-\\1\\b` (detects \"I-I\", \"The-the\").\n    *   **Repetition Regex**: `\\b(\\w+)\\s+\\1\\b` (detects \"I I\", \"The the\").\n    *   **Awkward Pause**: Calculated by `librosa.effects.split` using a **-25dB top_db** threshold. Any gap > 1.5s is flagged.\n*   **Tone Classification Heuristics**:\n    *   **🤩 Energetic**: High Energy (>0.06 RMS) + High Speed (>160 WPM).\n    *   **🧘 Calm/Confident**: Medium Energy (0.02–0.06 RMS) + Steady Pace (130-160 WPM).\n    *   **😰 Nervous**: Low Energy + High Pitch Variation (Shaky voice) + High Speed.\n\n---\n\n## 3. The \"AI Brain\": Multi-Frontier LLM Client\n\n### 3.1 Unified Routing Logic\n*   **Ollama Orchestration**: Connects via the `/api/chat` endpoint. Uses `temperature: 0.7` and `num_predict: 300` for concise, professional questions.\n*   **Cloud API Adapters**:\n    *   **Google Gemini**: Uses the `v1beta` registry. Wraps system instructions into the user role for 1.5 Flash/Pro compatibility.\n    *   **OpenAI O-Series**: Automatically detects `o1` or `o4` models via regex (`^o\\d`) and swaps `max_tokens` for **`max_completion_tokens`**.\n*   **Context Injection**:\n    *   **Resume/JD Integration**: Prepends the extracted text to the system prompt with clear headers: `[CONTEXT: RESUME]` and `[CONTEXT: JOB DESCRIPTION]`.\n\n---\n\n## 4. UI Innovation & Frontend Patterns\n\n### 4.1 Stability Mechanics\n*   **Isolated Fragments**: The **Unified Status Monitor** refreshes every 5s. To prevent this from resetting the audio recorder, the recorder is wrapped in its own `@st.fragment` with an independent `rec_nonce` state key.\n*   **Browser Audio Bypass**: Since standard `st.audio` often fails to autoplay on reruns, the system uses a **Base64 HTML Injector**:\n    *   Converts raw audio bytes to a B64 string.\n    *   Injects a hidden `<audio>` tag into the DOM.\n    *   Uses a JavaScript `<script>` to force `.play()` instantly.\n\n---\n\n## 5. Deployment & Data Schemas\n\n### 5.1 System Folder Architecture\n*   `/src/api`: Endpoint definitions and server security.\n*   `/src/backend`: The core AI \"Engines\" (Processor, Scorer, LLM Client).\n*   `/src/utils`: Utilities for PDF, History, and Text cleaning.\n*   `/temp_data`: Volatile storage for `.wav`, `.mp3`, and `.json` session data.\n*   `/logs`: Production debug logs with full traceback capture.\n\n### 5.2 History Schema (`session_history.json`)\n```json\n{\n    \"timestamp\": \"YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM\",\n    \"wpm\": 145,\n    \"fillers\": 2,\n    \"tone\": \"Confident\",\n    \"mode\": \"Technical Round\"\n}\n```\n\n### 5.3 Automated Provisioning (`install.py`)\n*   **Logic**: Uses `platform` and `struct` to determine if the system is 64-bit Windows.\n*   **GPU Detection**: Probes `nvidia-smi` and `torch.cuda`.\n*   **Package Picking**: \n    *   If **Python 3.13** on Windows: Pulls from `download.pytorch.org/whl/nightly/cu124`.\n    *   If **Mac**: Directs `pip` to install the Metal-optimized Torch.\n\n---\n**Status**: MVP Verified across Windows 11 (NVIDIA) and macOS (M2 Silicon).\n**Copyright**: Karanveer Singh, Amel Korandippillil Sunil, Orlando Santiago Cardenas Vargas.\n",

### Granular Details Added:
1.  **Threadpool Logic**: Detailed exactly why we use synchronous `def` functions in FastAPI to prevent blocking the event loop during heavy ML tasks.

3.  **DSP Algorithms**: Documented the **YIN Algorithm** for pitch tracking and the **-25dB threshold** used for awkward pause detection.
4.  **Tone Heuristics**: Provided the exact RMS Energy and WPM math used to classify emotions like "Energetic" vs. "Calm."
5.  **B64 Audio Hack**: Explained the JavaScript/Base64 injection method used to bypass browser autoplay restrictions.
6.  **O-Series Intelligence**: Documented the regex-based detection and prompt-wrapping required for newer OpenAI reasoning models.

  "args": {
    "file_path": "CONTEXT.md",
    "content": "# 🎙️ Master Project Context: AI-Powered Interview Coach (MVP)\n\nThis document is the definitive, all-encompassing source of truth for the AI Interview Coach project. It combines the original project vision, detailed functional capabilities, and granular technical specifications for final reports and presentations.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Project Vision & Identity\n*   **Mission**: To democratize elite professional coaching. High-quality interview preparation typically costs hundreds of dollars; this system provides that same level of personalized, data-driven feedback for free.\n*   **Privacy-First Philosophy**: Unlike cloud-only tools, this system is designed for **Local-First** execution. By using Ollama and Faster-Whisper, sensitive data like resumes and personal audio recordings can remain 100% offline, ensuring zero data leakage.\n*   **Core Innovation**: The system bridges the gap between **Semantic Intelligence** (understanding the content of your answers) and **Acoustic Analytics** (analyzing the quality of your speech delivery).\n\n---\n\n## 2. Technical Architecture: The Decoupled Hybrid Model\nThe application is built on a modular **Client-Server** architecture to ensure that heavy AI workloads never compromise the responsiveness of the user interface.\n\n### 2.1 Backend AI Hub (FastAPI)\n*   **Framework**: Python 3.11 - 3.13 served by **FastAPI** and **Uvicorn**.\n*   **Concurrency Strategy**: Computational routes (transcription, scoring, LLM generation) are implemented as synchronous `def` functions. This allows FastAPI to offload these heavy tasks to an external **ThreadPoolExecutor**, keeping the server responsive to status heartbeats even when the CPU/GPU is at 100% load.\n*   **Deployment Versatility**: Supports local execution, Docker containerization, and **Hybrid-Cloud** deployment. When running in hybrid mode, the backend is exposed via **ngrok**, allowing a cloud-hosted frontend to securely access local hardware.\n\n### 2.2 Frontend Command Center (Streamlit)\n*   **Reactive UI**: A modern dashboard that manages the entire interview lifecycle—from configuration and document parsing to live interaction and final reporting.\n*   **Stutter-Free Monitoring**: Uses `@st.fragment` to isolate the **Unified Status Monitor**. This allows the sidebar telemetry (CPU/RAM/VRAM) to refresh every few seconds without causing the \"grey-out\" effect or flickering the main chat interface.\n*   **Security Protocol**: Implements strict **X-Internal-Key** authentication. Every request between the frontend and backend is verified against a shared secret stored in the `.env` file or Streamlit Secrets.\n\n---\n\n## 3. The \"AI Ears\": Acoustic & Linguistic Pipeline\n\n### 3.1 Hardware-Aware Transcription (`AudioProcessor`)\n*   **Engine**: `faster-whisper` (utilizing the CTranslate2 C++ backend for 4x speed over standard Whisper).\n*   **Contextual Bias**: Uses a specialized initial prompt: *\"Umm, I-I think... well, actually... so your...\"* which forces the AI to transcribe natural filler words instead of filtering them out.\n*   **Hardware Intelligence**:\n    *   **NVIDIA GPU**: Uses CUDA with `medium.en` model (FP16 precision).\n    *   **Apple Silicon**: Optimizes for the **Apple Neural Engine (MPS)** using `medium.en` (FP32 precision).\n    *   **Standard CPU**: Falls back to `small.en` or `tiny.en` using **Int8 quantization** for stability on older hardware.\n*   **VRAM Management**: Implements a singleton model cache that clears memory and triggers garbage collection (`gc.collect()`) when switching between different compute modes.\n\n### 3.2 Deep Acoustic Scoring (`AcousticScorer`)\n*   **Signal Processing**: Leverages `librosa` to analyze raw audio at a 16kHz sample rate.\n*   **Pitch Analysis**: Uses the **YIN Algorithm** to track the fundamental frequency (F0). This measures \"Pitch Variation,\" identifying if a candidate sounds confident or monotone/shaky.\n*   **Energy Analysis**: Calculates **Root Mean Square (RMS)** energy to determine vocal volume and intensity.\n*   **Fluency Analytics (Regex)**:\n    *   **Fillers**: Detects phrases like `um`, `uh`, `ah`, `hmm`, `like`, `basically`.\n    *   **Stutters**: Identifies hyphenated repetitions (`\\b(\\w+)-\\1\\b`).\n    *   **Awkward Pauses**: Flags any silence longer than 1.5 seconds using a -25dB threshold.\n\n---\n\n## 4. The \"AI Brain\": Multi-Engine LLM Integration\n\n### 4.1 Unified LLM Client\n*   **Local Privacy**: Directly orchestrates **Ollama** via REST API for private sessions.\n*   **Cloud Frontier**: Full support for **OpenAI (GPT-4o)**, **Anthropic (Claude 3.5)**, and **Google Gemini (2.0 Flash)**.\n*   **Reasoning Model Support**: Specifically handles **OpenAI O-Series** (`o1`, `o4-mini`) by automatically swapping `max_tokens` for `max_completion_tokens` and wrapping system instructions into the user role.\n\n### 4.2 Context-Aware Personalization\n*   **Multi-Format Parsing**: Extracts text from `.pdf`, `.docx`, and `.txt` files using `pdfplumber` and `python-docx`.\n*   **Identity Extraction**: The AI scans the resume to find the candidate's name for personalized greetings.\n*   **Recruiter Impersonation**: If a hiring manager's name is found in the Job Description, the AI coach introduces itself as that specific person.\n*   **Cross-Referencing**: AI is instructed to look for inconsistencies in the candidate's answers based on their resume claims.\n\n---\n\n## 5. User Experience & Automation\n\n### 5.1 Gamified Dashboard\n*   **Performance Badges**: A logic-driven achievement system (e.g., \"Golden Pacer\" for ideal WPM, \"Silver Tongue\" for low filler counts).\n*   **Interactive Trends**: Uses **Plotly Express** to visualize pacing and fluency improvements over time.\n*   **STAR Framework**: Every answer is evaluated against the Situation, Task, Action, and Result framework for professional career coaching.\n\n### 5.2 Universal Automation (`install.py`)\n*   **Zero-Config Setup**: The installer scans CPU threads, RAM, and GPU vendors to install the \"Sweet Version\" of every dependency.\n*   **Portable Drivers**: Automatically downloads and configures **FFmpeg** binaries locally on Windows, eliminating the need for manual System PATH modifications.\n*   **Audio Autoplay Hack**: Injects Base64-encoded audio into a hidden HTML/JS component to bypass aggressive browser autoplay blocks.\n\n---\n**Data Drifters Team**: Karanveer Singh, Amel Korandippillil Sunil, Orlando Santiago Cardenas Vargas.\n**Project Status**: MVP Fully Verified and Documented.\n",

### 1. Update `src/utils/text_processor.py`
I'll add a new `KeywordExtractor` that identifies "Power Words" and "Technical Skills" from any text.**Tool Command**:

Standard Technical Keyword Database (Expansion point)\n    tech_keywords = {\n        \"python\", \"javascript\", \"react\", \"fastapi\", \"docker\", \"kubernetes\", \"aws\", \"azure\", \n        \"sql\", \"nosql\", \"ci/cd\", \"agile\", \"scrum\", \"rest api\", \"graphql\", \"machine learning\",\n        \"nlp\", \"security\", \"encryption\", \"iso 27001\", \"wazuh\", \"siem\", \"linux\", \"cloud\"\n    }\n    \n    # 2. Extract words and clean\n    words = set(re.findall(r'\\b[a-z]{2,}\\b', text.lower()))\n    \n    # 3. Find matches\n    found = words.intersection(tech_keywords)\n    return sorted(list(found))\n\ndef calculate_keyword_match(transcript, target_keywords):\n    \"\"\"Returns the percentage of target keywords mentioned in the transcript.\"\"\"\n    if not target_keywords: return 0\n    transcript_words = set(re.findall(r'\\b[a-z]{2,}\\b', transcript.lower()))\n    matches = transcript_words.intersection(set(target_keywords))\n    return int((len(matches) / len(target_keywords)) * 100)\n",
    "file_path": "src/utils/text_processor.py"
  },
  "id": "replace_1776119544423_0"
}

It combines the project vision, functional capabilities, and granular technical specifications for final reports, engineering documentation, and MVP presentations.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Project Vision & Identity\n*   **Mission**: To democratize elite professional coaching. High-quality interview preparation typically costs hundreds of dollars; this system provides that same level of personalized, data-driven feedback for free.\n*   **Privacy-First Philosophy**: Designed for **Local-First** execution. By using Ollama and Faster-Whisper, sensitive data like resumes and personal audio recordings remain 100% offline, ensuring zero data leakage.\n*   **Core Innovation**: The system bridges the gap between **Semantic Intelligence** (understanding *what* you say) and **Acoustic Analytics** (analyzing *how* you say it).\n\n---\n\n## 2. Technical Architecture: The Decoupled Hybrid Model\nThe application is built on a modular **Client-Server** architecture to ensure that heavy AI workloads never compromise the responsiveness of the user interface.\n\n### 2.1 Backend AI Hub (FastAPI)\n*   **Framework**: Python 3.11 - 3.13 served by **FastAPI** and **Uvicorn**.\n*   **Concurrency Strategy**: Computational routes (transcription, scoring, LLM generation) are implemented as synchronous `def` functions. This allows FastAPI to offload these heavy tasks to an external **ThreadPoolExecutor**, keeping the server responsive even when the hardware is at 100% load.\n*   **Security (Inter-Service Auth)**: Implements a mandatory **X-Internal-Key** authentication layer. Every request from the frontend is verified against a shared secret stored in the host environment to prevent unauthorized access to local AI engines.\n*   **Hybrid Connectivity**: Specifically optimized for **ngrok** tunnels. By using JSON POST requests for long-form data (Resume/JD), the system avoids the strict URL length limits of proxy services.\n\n### 2.2 Frontend Command Center (Streamlit)\n*   **Stutter-Free Heartbeat**: Uses `@st.fragment` to isolate the **Unified Status Monitor**. This allows sidebar telemetry (CPU/RAM/VRAM) to refresh every 2 seconds without triggering a full page refresh, preventing UI flicker.\n*   **Audio Autoplay Implementation**: Bypasses aggressive browser \"autoplay blocks\" by converting audio bytes to **Base64** and injecting them into a hidden HTML `<audio>` component with a **nonce-based unique key**, forcing an instant trigger.\n*   **Persistent State**: Utilizes `st.session_state` to track the 3-step setup wizard, preserving configurations and chat history across tab navigations.\n\n---\n\n## 3. The \"AI Ears\": High-Fidelity Acoustic Pipeline\n\n### 3.1 Hardware-Aware Transcription (`AudioProcessor`)\n*   **Engine**: `faster-whisper` (utilizing the CTranslate2 C++ backend for 4x speed over standard Whisper).\n*   **Configuration Parameters**:\n    *   **Beam Size**: 5 (Optimal balance of accuracy and latency).\n    *   **Bias Prompt**: *\"Umm, I-I think... well, actually... so your...\"* (Forces the AI to transcribe filler words instead of \"cleaning\" them).\n*   **Precision Intelligence**:\n    *   **NVIDIA GPU**: Uses CUDA with `medium.en` model (Int8_Float16 precision).\n    *   **Apple Silicon**: Detects M-series chips and optimizes for the **Apple Neural Engine** using `medium.en` (Float32 precision).\n    *   **Standard CPU**: Falls back to `small.en` with **Int8 quantization** for stability.\n\n### 3.2 Deep Acoustic Scoring (`AcousticScorer`)\n*   **Signal Processing**: Leverages `librosa` at a **16kHz sample rate**.\n*   **Pitch Analysis**: Uses the **YIN Algorithm** for fundamental frequency (F0) tracking. This measures \"Pitch Variation\"—a key indicator of confidence vs. nervousness.\n*   **Tone Classification Heuristics**:\n    *   **🤩 Energetic**: High Energy (>0.06 RMS) + High Speed (>160 WPM).\n    *   **🧘 Calm/Confident**: Medium Energy (0.02–0.06 RMS) + Steady Pace (130-160 WPM).\n    *   **😰 Nervous**: Low Energy + High Pitch Variation (Shaky voice) + High Speed.\n*   **Fluency Engine (Regex)**:\n    *   **Fillers**: `\\b(um+?|uh+?|ah+?|hmm+|like|basically|actually)\\b`.\n    *   **Stutters**: `\\b(\\w+)-\\1\\b` (detects \"I-I\", \"The-the\").\n    *   **Pauses**: Identifies silences > 1.5 seconds using energy thresholds.\n\n---\n\n## 4. The \"AI Brain\": Multi-Frontier LLM Integration\n\n### 4.1 Unified LLM Client\n*   **Ollama Orchestration**: Direct REST integration using `temperature: 0.7` and `num_predict: 300` for concise responses.\n*   **Cloud Frontier**: Full support for **OpenAI (GPT-4o)**, **Anthropic (Claude 3.5)**, and **Google Gemini (2.0 Flash)**.\n*   **Reasoning Model Support**: Automatically detects **OpenAI O-Series** (`o1`, `o4-mini`) via regex (`^o\\d`). It automatically:\n    *   Swaps `max_tokens` for **`max_completion_tokens`**.\n    *   Wraps system instructions into the `user` role (to accommodate models that reject the `system` role).\n\n### 4.2 Context-Aware Personalization\n*   **Multi-Format Parsing**: Extracts text from `.pdf`, `.docx`, and `.txt` files using `pdfplumber` and `python-docx`.\n*   **Identity Extraction**: The AI scans the resume to greet the candidate by name. If a recruiter's name is found in the Job Description, the AI adopts that specific identity.\n*   **Cross-Referencing**: AI is specifically instructed to cross-reference answers against resume claims to look for inconsistencies or overstatements.\n\n---\n\n## 5. Reporting & Gamification\n\n### 5.1 Dashboard Analytics\n*   **Performance Badges**: A logic-driven achievement system:\n    *   **🎓 Master Interviewer**: 10+ sessions completed.\n    *   **🎯 Golden Pacer**: Average WPM between 130 and 160.\n    *   **✨ Silver Tongue**: Average filler word count < 2 per turn.\n*   **Trend Tracking**: Uses **Plotly Express** to render interactive time-series charts showing the user's improvement in pacing and fluency over time.\n\n### 5.2 STAR Framework Evaluation\nThe coach provides a deep-dive evaluation strictly following the **Situation, Task, Action, and Result** framework, ensuring feedback is structured like a professional performance review.\n\n---\n\n## 6. Deployment & Automation Innovation\n\n### 6.1 Universal Automation (`install.py`)\n*   **Intelligent Provisioning**: Scans CPU threads, RAM, and GPU vendors to install the **\"Sweet Version\"** of every dependency (e.g., handling specialized Nightly CUDA 12.4 builds for Python 3.13 on Windows).\n*   **Portable Drivers**: Automatically downloads and configures **FFmpeg** binaries locally on Windows, eliminating the need for manual System PATH modifications.\n\n### 6.2 Universal Launchers\n*   `start.bat` (Windows) and `start.sh` (Mac/Linux) provide a \"double-click\" experience, handling environment creation, dependency updates, and multi-service orchestration simultaneously.\n\n---\n**Data Drifters Team**: Karanveer Singh, Amel Korandippillil Sunil, Orlando Santiago Cardenas Vargas.\n**Project Status**: MVP Fully Verified and Documented.\n",
