The \"AI Brain\": Multi-Frontier LLM Integration\n\n### 4.1 Unified LLM Client\n*   **Ollama Orchestration**: Direct REST integration using `temperature: 0.7` and `num_predict: 300` for concise responses.\n*   **Cloud Frontier**: Full support for **OpenAI (GPT-4o)**, **Anthropic (Claude 3.5)**, and **Google Gemini (2.0 Flash)**.\n*   **Reasoning Model Support**: Automatically detects **OpenAI O-Series** (`o1`, `o4-mini`) via regex (`^o\\d`). It automatically:\n    *   Swaps `max_tokens` for **`max_completion_tokens`**.\n    *   Wraps system instructions into the `user` role (to accommodate models that reject the `system` role).\n\n### 4.2 Context-Aware Personalization\n*   **Multi-Format Parsing**: Extracts text from `.pdf`, `.docx`, and `.txt` files using `pdfplumber` and `python-docx`.\n*   **Identity Extraction**: The AI scans the resume to greet the candidate by name. If a recruiter's name is found in the Job Description, the AI adopts that specific identity.\n*   **Cross-Referencing**: AI is specifically instructed to cross-reference answers against resume claims to look for inconsistencies or overstatements.\n\n---\n\n## 5. Reporting & Gamification\n\n### 5.1 Dashboard Analytics\n*   **Performance Badges**: A logic-driven achievement system:\n    *   **🎓 Master Interviewer**: 10+ sessions completed.\n    *   **🎯 Golden Pacer**: Average WPM between 130 and 160.\n    *   **✨ Silver Tongue**: Average filler word count < 2 per turn.\n*   **Trend Tracking**: Uses **Plotly Express** to render interactive time-series charts showing the user's improvement in pacing and fluency over time.\n\n### 5.2 STAR Framework Evaluation\nThe coach provides a deep-dive evaluation strictly following the **Situation, Task, Action, and Result** framework, ensuring feedback is structured like a professional performance review.\n\n---\n\n## 6. Deployment & Automation Innovation\n\n### 6.1 Universal Automation (`install.py`)\n*   **Intelligent Provisioning**: Scans CPU threads, RAM, and GPU vendors to install the **\"Sweet Version\"** of every dependency (e.g., handling specialized Nightly CUDA 12.4 builds for Python 3.13 on Windows).\n*   **Portable Drivers**: Automatically downloads and configures **FFmpeg** binaries locally on Windows, eliminating the need for manual System PATH modifications.\n\n### 6.2 Universal Launchers\n*   `start.bat` (Windows) and `start.sh` (Mac/Linux) provide a \"double-click\" experience, handling environment creation, dependency updates, and multi-service orchestration simultaneously.\n\n---\n**Data Drifters Team**: Karanveer Singh, Amel Korandippillil Sunil, Orlando Santiago Cardenas Vargas.\n**Project Status**: MVP Fully Verified and Documented.\n"
  }
}

*   **`src/api/client.py`**: ~130 lines (Streamlit-to-FastAPI bridge and header authentication)

### 🧠 Core AI Engines (~650 Lines)
*   **`src/backend/llm_client.py`**: ~210 lines (Ollama, OpenAI, Anthropic, Gemini routing & logic)
*   **`src/backend/scorer.py`**: ~150 lines (Librosa pitch/energy analysis and Regex fluency tracking)
*   **`src/backend/audio_processor.py`**: ~130 lines (Faster-Whisper loading, caching, and transcription)
*   **`src/backend/personas.py`**: ~100 lines (System prompts, persona data, and recruiter impersonation)
*   **`src/backend/hardware.py`**: ~60 lines (Apple Silicon, CPU, and NVIDIA detection logic)

*   **`src/utils/diagnostics.py`**: ~140 lines (Global crash protection, Windows DLL mapping, and logging)
*   **`src/utils/pdf_generator.py`**: ~110 lines (HTML-to-PDF conversion and Unicode sanitization)

### 🧪 Testing Suite (~170 Lines)

*   **`tests/stress_test.py`**: ~55 lines (Asyncio simulated load testing)

    "content": "# Plan: Fix Docker Build Connection Errors\n\nThe Docker build for the API component is failing because `apt-get update` cannot reliably connect to the Ubuntu mirrors, leading to broken package dependencies and a failed installation of `ffmpeg` and `curl`.\n\n## Objective\nIncrease the robustness of the package installation process in `Dockerfile.api` to handle transient network failures and ensure a consistent package state before installation.\n\n## Key Files & Context\n- **File**: `Dockerfile.api`\n- **Context**: The build fails at step 3/6 with exit code 100 due to unmet dependencies and \"held broken packages,\" which is a direct consequence of a partially failed `apt-get update`.\n\n## Implementation Steps\n1. **Modify `Dockerfile.api`**:\n    - Replace the standard `RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y ...` with a more resilient command.\n    - Implement a retry loop for `apt-get update` (up to 3 attempts with increasing sleep intervals).\n    - Add the `--fix-missing` flag to `apt-get install` to allow the build to proceed if some packages are already available or can be fetched from alternative paths.\n    - Consolidate the commands into a single `RUN` layer to minimize image size and ensure cleanups are performed.\n\n## Verification & Testing\n1. **Trigger Build**: Run `docker-compose up --build`.\n2. **Observe Logs**: Verify that if the first update attempt fails, the script retries and eventually succeeds.\n3. **Verify Installation**: Once the build completes, ensure the container starts and the `ffmpeg` and `curl` binaries are functional (via healthchecks).\n",
    "file_path": "C:\\Users\\karan\\.gemini\\tmp\\ai-powered-interview-coaching-system\\847ad650-e098-4ed9-abf7-0368576ca983\\plans/fix-docker-build-errors.md"

}
